The following are the **scheduled events** of **association football** for the **calendar year 2024** throughout the world. This includes:

* In countries whose league seasons fall within a single calendar year, the **2024 season**.
* In countries which crown one champion in a season that spans two calendar years, the **2023–24 season**.
* In countries which split their league season into two championships, a system often known in Latin America as [Apertura and Clausura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apertura_and_Clausura "Apertura and Clausura"), all championships **awarded in calendar 2024**.

* 15–25 February: [2024 FIFA Beach Soccer World Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024_FIFA_Beach_Soccer_World_Cup "2024 FIFA Beach Soccer World Cup") in ![](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/cb/Flag_of_the_United_Arab_Emirates.svg/40px-Flag_of_the_United_Arab_Emirates.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail) [United Arab Emirates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Arab_Emirates "United Arab Emirates")
  + ![1st place, gold medalist(s)](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/47/Gold_medal_icon.svg/20px-Gold_medal_icon.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail): ![](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/0/05/Flag_of_Brazil.svg/40px-Flag_of_Brazil.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail) [Brazil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazil_national_beach_soccer_team "Brazil national beach soccer team")
  + ![2nd place, silver medalist(s)](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Silver_medal_icon.svg/20px-Silver_medal_icon.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail): ![](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/0/03/Flag_of_Italy.svg/40px-Flag_of_Italy.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail) [Italy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italy_national_beach_soccer_team "Italy national beach soccer team")
  + ![3rd place, bronze medalist(s)](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/89/Bronze_medal_icon.svg/20px-Bronze_medal_icon.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail): ![](//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/ca/Flag_of_Iran.svg/40px-Flag_of_Iran.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail) [Iran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran_national_beach_soccer_team "Iran national beach soccer team")

The team that tops the league table is designated "premiers".
